# About this item

*    【SUPER CAPACITY AND PEAK】The NEXPOW Upgraded Auto Car Jump Starter uses a high-speed polymer battery instead of a normal starter, which provides super capacity to easily start a 12V vehicle up to 20 times (up to 7.0L gas or 6.5L diesel engine). Even if the car battery is dead, it also can restart the vehicle. Moreover, the battery jump starter can start trucks, motorcycles, snowmobiles, ATVs, UTV, lawnmowers up in a few minutes, even in extreme temperatures (-4°F~140°F). 
*    【MULTI- FUNCTION EMERGENCY EQUIPMENT】Emergency jump starter + portable charger + LED Flashlight + S.O.S Lighting Signal for help + Dual USB quick charge +for the vehicle electrical supply. A Must Have For Every Car! 
*    【SUPER CAPACITY POWER BANK】Battery booster With super capacity and Two USB Output Ports (one is 5V/9V/12V USB Quick Charge), the car battery jump box can fully charge your laptops, smartphones, tablets, and other devices at the fastest speed possible. And you can charge two devices at the same time. 
*    【IMPROVED SAFETY TECHNOLOGY】NEXPOW portable car battery jump starters have Intelligent jump cables built-in 8 upgraded protections, particularly spark-proof, reverse polarity protection, high-temperature protection, over-load protection. The car battery jump box/jump cable flashing light and beep will tell you wrong operations. 
*    【WHAT YOU GET】You will receive a whole set of G17 auto battery charger jump starter, smart jumper cables, USB Type-C cable, user manual, 15W adaptor and a delicate carrying case. 
*    【TIPS】 If you have not used the jump starter for a long time, it is recommended to charge it every 2-3 months to keep it in a healthy state. Operating temperature : -20℃ ~ 60℃ （ -4℉ ~ 140℉ ). Don't use the product at a high temperature or place it for a long time in a high-temperature environment. Do not jump start the car at high frequency continuously.

Global Recycled Standard (GRS) certified products contain recycled content (>50%) that has been independently verified at each stage of the supply chain, from the source to the final product and meet social, environmental, and chemical requirements. The purchase of Global Recycled Standard (GRS) certified products supports the use of recycled content in the supply chain.

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the jump starter to be a fantastic product that works right out of the box and is much easier to use than jumper cables. They appreciate its compact size that's perfect for storage in any vehicle, and the included carrying case receives positive feedback for its quality. The device provides plenty of power for jump starting, with one customer noting it's powerful enough to start a Chevy 5.3-liter engine. Battery life experiences are mixed, with some reporting it's fully charged while others say it won't hold a charge.

# Product Summary: NEXPOW Car Jump Starter, 2000A Peak 12V Portable Car Battery Starter, Auto Battery Booster, Lithium Jump Box with LED Light/USB Quick Charge 3.0

From NEXPOW

4.6 out of 5 stars, 8,775 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B07T9S3Q5N?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)
50+ viewed in past week

## About this Item

*   【SUPER CAPACITY AND PEAK】The NEXPOW Upgraded Auto Car Jump Starter uses a high-speed polymer battery instead of a normal starter, which provides super capacity to easily start a 12V vehicle up to 20 times (up to 7.0L gas or 6.5L diesel engine). Even if the car battery is dead, it also can restart the vehicle. Moreover, the battery jump starter can start trucks, motorcycles, snowmobiles, ATVs, UTV, lawnmowers up in a few minutes, even in extreme temperatures (-4°F~140°F).
*   【MULTI- FUNCTION EMERGENCY EQUIPMENT】Emergency jump starter + portable charger + LED Flashlight + S.O.S Lighting Signal for help + Dual USB quick charge +for the vehicle electrical supply. A Must Have For Every Car!
*   【SUPER CAPACITY POWER BANK】Battery booster With super capacity and Two USB Output Ports (one is 5V/9V/12V USB Quick Charge), the car battery jump box can fully charge your laptops, smartphones, tablets, and other devices at the fastest speed possible. And you can charge two devices at the same time.
*   【IMPROVED SAFETY TECHNOLOGY】NEXPOW portable car battery jump starters have Intelligent jump cables built-in 8 upgraded protections, particularly spark-proof, reverse polarity protection, high-temperature protection, over-load protection. The car battery jump box/jump cable flashing light and beep will tell you wrong operations.
*   【WHAT YOU GET】You will receive a whole set of G17 auto battery charger jump starter, smart jumper cables, USB Type-C cable, user manual, 15W adaptor and a delicate carrying case.
*   【TIPS】 If you have not used the jump starter for a long time, it is recommended to charge it every 2-3 months to keep it in a healthy state. Operating temperature : -20℃ ~ 60℃ （ -4℉ ~ 140℉ ). Don't use the product at a high temperature or place it for a long time in a high-temperature environment. Do not jump start the car at high frequency continuously.
